    59	
    60	The operator then chose "New rule, fresh runs". A rule script, `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`, was committed with its scores on the twelve earlier packets before any fresh run: a frozen anchor missing from a packet counts unless it is an optional Level 3 `tasks.md` anchor whose fill-note condition holds. The first swe-2-max final set scores 0, 0 and 3 under it and the second DeepSeek set scores 0, 0 and 0. Three fresh runs then ran on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` in a disposable worktree with this packet's folder removed from its working tree. All three passed strict validation with no leftover hints, but run 1 deleted the whole required `fix-completeness` section, so counted `missing_anchors` is 1, 0 and 0 against a baseline maximum of 0 and criterion 5 fails for the third time. Run 3's deleted sections (`deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `sign-off`) are excused by the rule.
    61	
    62	### Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout
    63	
    64	The closeout also published the benchmark report with verdict FAIL, wrote the global changelog `v2.9.0.0` and bumped the version carrier, wrote the nested changelogs for phases 001 to 003 and this phase, regenerated the trigger index, confirmed the track roots and reconciled the parent phase map and goal log. `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` holds one line for each parent criterion: six PASS lines and one FAIL line, `C5 FAIL`.

    86	## How It Was Delivered
    87	
    88	One Claude Code worker ran the phase and spawned no subagent. It ran three serial cli-devin swe-2-max dispatches of the frozen Level 3 brief and audited each run's Devin session. It measured each run with the five commands recorded in `scratch/tools/run-final.sh`, wrote every number in the report from `delta.json` and the run files, and proved each parent criterion before ticking any box. The census for criterion 3 was rerun over the frozen folder list, which excludes this packet. One read-only frontier review on cli-codex gpt-6-luna read the closeout diff, and each finding is recorded in `goal.md` section 4. The operator moved the review from the cli-devin swe-2-max that parent D4 names.
    89	
    90	### Operator handoff
    91	
    92	- This phase made one local commit on the worktree branch. Nothing was pushed.
    93	- The label fix shipped and the rerun failed criterion 5 again. The open decision is the operator's: whether `missing_anchors` should count the optional Level 3 verification sections, or whether the delete-when clauses in those fill notes should change, before any further rerun.
    94	- The parent `spec.md` and this phase are In Progress, not Complete, until a rerun passes criterion 5.
    95	- No generated artifact needs a hand edit before review.

    99	
   100	<!-- ANCHOR:decisions -->
   101	## Key Decisions
   102	
   103	| Decision | Why |
   104	|----------|-----|
   105	| Keep the frozen metric and the FAIL, and add a labelled diagnostic | The operator chose "Option 3 + diagnostic". Changing the metric after the result would hide the finding |
   106	| Count bare hints plus value tokens for M2 | The two patterns are disjoint, so each unfilled instruction counts once on both sides |
   107	| Use Level 3 for the final runs | The SWE 2 Max baseline is Level 3 |
   108	| Prove parent criterion 3 on the frozen folder list | A live walk would compare different populations |
   109	| Keep the first final set as history and label the second set as the final one | The sets differ in model and tree, so each is reported with its own baseline |
   110	| Score every earlier set under the new rule before any fresh run | The operator's row claims two scores, and the script had to reproduce them first |
   111	| Keep a contaminated run in the numbers | Dropping it would hide what the session read, and the verdict does not depend on it |
   112	| Do not mark the phase or the parent Complete | The validator reads a Complete status with an unmet acceptance row as a failed closure |
   113	<!-- /ANCHOR:decisions -->

   133	
   134	<!-- ANCHOR:limitations -->
   135	## Known Limitations
   136	
   137	1. **Parent criterion 5 is FAIL on all three final sets.** `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 6 against 0 on the first set, 3 against 0 on the second and, counted under the new rule, 1 against 0 on the fresh set, and the phase and the parent stay In Progress.
   138	2. **Open decision.** The required `fix-completeness` anchor is missing from a passing packet because strict validation does not require it and its fill note invites deleting rows. Decide whether to enforce the required anchors, move that section to the optional set with a condition, or reword the note, then rerun in a fresh disposable worktree as the fresh set did.
   139	3. **Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim.** One run produced the whole failure, so the sample cannot separate the template change from run-to-run variation.
   140	4. **The fixture is one small feature and four documents.** A different feature shape or a Level 1 packet is not measured.
   141	5. **Acceptance rows AC-010, AC-011 and AC-015 are Unmet.** They need nine Complete map rows, seven PASS lines and a Complete status, which criterion 5 prevents.

   149	- [x] T007 Write the delta for the five metrics (`scratch/baseline/delta.json`, `scratch/baseline/delta.md`). Run the block below. Verify: `jq '.metrics | length' "$SC/baseline/delta.json"` prints `5`, `grep -cE '^\| (strict_passed|leftover_hints|missing_anchors|out_of_scope_writes|seconds) \|' "$SC/baseline/delta.md"` prints `5`, and the last command prints `true`. When it prints `false`, write `C5 FAILED: a final run failed strict validation or is worse than the baseline's worst on leftover_hints, missing_anchors or out_of_scope_writes` in `goal.md` section 4, run T008 only, then write `STOP` in `goal.md` section 4 and stop the closeout. Do not tick the parent criterion and do not publish a changelog.
   150	
   151	  ```bash
   152	  cd "$WORKTREE"
   153	  B="$BL/baseline.json"
   154	  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
   155	  jq -n --slurpfile b "$B" --slurpfile f "$F" '
   156	    def stat($runs; $k):
   157	      {min: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | min),
   158	       max: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | max),
   159	       mean: (($runs | map(.[$k]) | add) / ($runs | length))};
   160	    ["strict_passed","leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes","seconds"] as $keys
   161	    | {metrics: ($keys | map(. as $k | {metric: $k, baseline: stat($b[0].runs; $k), final: stat($f[0].runs; $k)}))}
   162	    | .metrics |= map(. + {delta_mean: (.final.mean - .baseline.mean)})
   163	  ' > "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
   164	  jq -r '
   165	    def one: (.*10 | round) / 10;
   166	    "# Authoring delta\n",
   167	    "- Baseline: three Level 3 runs of the frozen brief on cli-devin swe-2-max, recorded in `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/baseline.json`.",
   168	    "- Final: three runs of the same task, recorded in `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`.",
   169	    "- Each cell is the minimum, the maximum and the mean over three runs.",
   170	    "- Three runs cannot support a significance claim. The table reports a range, not an effect size.\n",
   171	    "| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |",
   172	    "|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|",
   173	    (.metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |")
   174	  ' "$SC/baseline/delta.json" > "$SC/baseline/delta.md"
   175	  jq -e 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max))' "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
   176	  ```

Read `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/spec.md`, `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/plan.md` and `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/implementation-summary.md` for the report shape phase 8 accepted, and read `.skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-benchmark/SKILL.md` sections 3 and 5 for the required package shape and the report contract, before composing the report. Run the block below, which writes `results.csv`, `README.md` and `source.md` from `delta.json` and inserts one index row. Verify: `awk 'NR>1' "$REPORT/results.csv" | wc -l` prints `6`, `grep -c '^## [0-9]' "$REPORT/README.md"` prints `10`, `grep -c 'model-eval--authoring-baseline' "$S/benchmark/reports/README.md"` prints `1`, and the script prints `NAME_OK`.
   179	
   180	  ```bash
   181	  cd "$WORKTREE"
   182	  DATE=$(date +%F)
   183	  NAME="$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline"
   184	  REPORT="$S/benchmark/reports/$NAME"
   185	  DELTA="$SC/baseline/delta.json"
   186	  B="$BL/baseline.json"
   187	  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
   188	  echo "$NAME" | grep -qE '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$' && echo NAME_OK
   189	  mkdir -p "$REPORT"
   190	  {
   191	    echo 'side,run,strict_passed,leftover_hints,missing_anchors,out_of_scope_writes,seconds'
   192	    jq -r '.runs[] | "baseline,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$B"
   193	    jq -r '.runs[] | "final,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$F"
   194	  } > "$REPORT/results.csv"
   195	  val() { jq -r --arg m "$1" --arg s "$2" --arg k "$3" '.metrics[] | select(.metric==$m) | .[$s][$k]' "$DELTA"; }
   196	  L_B_MIN=$(val leftover_hints baseline min); L_B_MAX=$(val leftover_hints baseline max)
   197	  L_F_MIN=$(val leftover_hints final min);   L_F_MAX=$(val leftover_hints final max)
   198	  VERDICT=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "PASS" else "FAIL" end' "$DELTA")
   199	  {
   200	    printf '# %s\n\n' "$NAME"
   201	    printf '> system-spec-kit · model-eval · authoring-baseline · cli-devin swe-2-max\n\n---\n\n'
   202	    printf '## 1. HEADLINE / OVERVIEW\n\n'
   203	    printf '**Verdict: %s**\n\n' "$VERDICT"
   204	    printf 'Three baseline runs and three final runs of one frozen Level 3 authoring brief. Leftover hints run from %s to %s on the baseline side and from %s to %s on the final side. The verdict is PASS when every final run passes strict validation and none is worse than the worst baseline run on leftover hints, missing anchors or out-of-scope writes.\n\n' "$L_B_MIN" "$L_B_MAX" "$L_F_MIN" "$L_F_MAX"
   205	    printf '## 2. AGGREGATE RESULTS\n\n'
   206	    echo '| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |'
   207	    echo '|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|'
   208	    jq -r 'def one: (.*10 | round) / 10; .metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |"' "$DELTA"
   209	    printf '\n## 3. METHODOLOGY\n\n'
   210	    cat <<'MD' | sed 's/^  //'
   211	  Fixture: one Level 3 packet for a `--quiet` flag on `recommend-level.sh`. The brief is frozen and copied from the phase 1 baseline with no word changed. Each side is three serial runs by cli-devin `swe-2-max`, and each run's Devin session is audited for reads of baseline evidence. The measured documents are `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` and `acceptance-criteria.md`. The throwaway track is deleted after each measurement.
   212	
   213	  ## 4. PER-CANDIDATE PROFILES
   214	
   215	  | Side | Tree | What the worker saw |
   216	  |---|---|---|
   217	  | baseline | Before the packet changes | v2.2 templates, bare bracketed hints, the hand-written examples |
   218	  | final | After phases 1 to 8 | v2.3 value tokens, section fill guidance, the authoring card, the guards |
   219	
   220	  ## 5. PROCESS NOTES
   221	
   222	  The baseline side ran in phase 1 on templates identical to the start commit and was not rerun. The final side ran after every phase closed, so the worker saw the tree the packet ships. A run that left its track behind was restored before the next dispatch.
   223	
   224	  ## 6. FINDINGS
   225	
   226	  The load-bearing numbers are the worst final run on leftover hints, missing anchors and out-of-scope writes, read against the worst baseline run. The baseline already reached 0 hints in one run, so the packet claims no regression rather than a lower minimum. A wide final range means the templates help some runs and not others.
   227	
   228	  ## 7. CAVEATS
   229	
   230	  Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim. The fixture is one small feature and four documents. The model is one model at one effort. A template change and a card change landed together, so this report cannot separate their effects.
   231	
   232	  ## 8. RECOMMENDATIONS
   233	
   234	  - **Apply now.** Keep the v2.3 token gate and the card. Both are measured on the same task.
   235	  - **Validate first.** Rerun this report after the next template change with a new date and the same variant.
   236	  - **Future.** Add a second feature shape if a future packet needs to know whether the gain holds outside one small feature.
   237

   239	
   240	MD
   241	    printf '%s\n' '```bash' \
   242	      'bash .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 3 --track zz-bench-hints --short-name recommend-level-quiet-flag "Add a quiet flag to recommend-level.sh"' \
   243	      'rg -o --no-filename "\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]" specs/zz-bench-hints/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag/*.md | sort -u > hints.txt' \
   244	      '```'
   245	    printf '\nThe three final runs take about 20 minutes, against 17 minutes on the baseline side. The metric commands are in `tasks.md` T006. The raw run logs stay in the source packet named in `source.md`.\n\n'
   246	    printf '## 10. CROSS-LINKS\n\n'
   247	    printf -- '- Source packet: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring`\n'
   248	    printf -- '- Baseline measurement: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/baseline.json`\n'
   249	    printf -- '- Delta: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/baseline/delta.json`\n'
   250	  } > "$REPORT/README.md"
   251	  {
   252	    printf '# system-spec-kit Benchmark Sources\n\n'
   253	    printf '> system-spec-kit · model-eval · authoring-baseline\n\n---\n\n'
   254	    printf '## 1. OVERVIEW\n\n'
   255	    printf 'This map names where the raw evidence behind this folder lives. The folder holds curated outputs only.\n\n'
   256	    printf '| Resource | Purpose |\n|---|---|\n'
   257	    printf '| Target skill | `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit` |\n'
   258	    printf '| Source packet | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring` |\n'
   259	    printf '| Baseline runs | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/baseline.json` |\n'
   260	    printf '| Final runs | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/baseline/baseline.json` |\n'
   261	    printf '| Metric commands | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/tasks.md` T006 |\n'
   262	    printf '| Delta | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/baseline/delta.json` |\n'
   263	    printf '| Run briefs | `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/briefs/T005-*.txt` |\n'
   264	    printf '| Model | cli-devin `swe-2-max` |\n\n'
   265	    printf '## 2. BOUNDARY\n\n'
   266	    printf 'The corpus is an input and is never rewritten by a run. This folder holds outputs only, and a run whose result changes gets a new folder rather than overwriting this one.\n\n'
   267	    printf 'Last updated %s.\n' "$DATE"
   268	  } > "$REPORT/source.md"
   269	  ROW="| $DATE | [\`$NAME/\`](./$NAME/) | cli-devin swe-2-max, frozen Level 3 authoring brief | 3 baseline and 3 final runs, 5 metrics | **$VERDICT** | \`054-weak-model-spec-authoring\` |"
   270	  ROW="$ROW" perl -pi -e 'if (/^## 2\. RUN INDEX/) { $in = 1 } if ($in && /^\|---\|/ && !$done) { $_ .= "$ENV{ROW}\n"; $done = 1 }' "$S/benchmark/reports/README.md"
   271	  echo INDEX_ROW_ADDED
   272	  python3 .skilled/skills/sk-doc/shared/scripts/validate_document.py "$REPORT/README.md" | tail -2
   273	  ```

   290	
   291	  # v2.9.0.0, Templates a Weak Model Can Follow
   292	
   293	  A weak model can now see what a template still needs, because every hint in the five core templates is a value token that fails validation once the packet leaves Draft. The four authoring intents also load a short card rather than the long guide, and the examples no longer teach the defects the old text carried.
   294	
   295	  > Spec folder: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring` (Level 2)
   296	
   297	  &nbsp;
   298	
   299	  ## What's New at a Glance
   300	
   301	  - **An unfilled hint can no longer pass as finished.** A doc stamped v2.3 warns while the spec is Draft and fails strict validation at any other status.
   302	  - **Every core section says how to fill it.** Each section carries a short instruction and a good and weak example.
   303	  - **Authoring starts on one page.** The router loads a short card for the four authoring intents.
   304	  - **The examples stop teaching the old defects.** Their descriptions, stale checklist lines and anchors now match the templates.
   305	  - **Commands and agents name the new gate and the card.** A speckit or doctor run describes the same authoring path a worker follows.
   306	  - **The playbook and catalog cover the new behavior.** A focused run report records each scenario result.
   307	  - **The result is measured, not asserted.** Three runs of one frozen authoring task on each side give a range for five metrics.
   308	
   309	  &nbsp;
   310	
   311	  ## Upgrade
   312	
   313	  No migration required. Existing packets keep their verdict, because the token gate applies only to docs stamped v2.3. Scaffold a new packet to get the new templates and the authoring card.

   540	  ```bash
   541	  cd "$WORKTREE"
   542	  C="$SC/closeout/criteria.txt"
   543	  : > "$C"
   544	
   545	  # C1: recursive strict on the packet.
   546	  OUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1)
   547	  if [ "$OUT" = "RESULT: PASSED" ]; then echo "C1 PASS $OUT" >> "$C"; else echo "C1 FAIL $OUT" >> "$C"; fi
   548	
   549	  # C2: the token gate on a fresh v2.3 packet and on an old stamp.
   550	  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
   551	  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 1 --track zz-bench-closeout --short-name gate "Gate" > "$SC/closeout/c2-create.txt" 2>&1

   557	  perl -pi -e 's/\| v2\.3 -->/| v2.2 -->/' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md

   559	  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
   560	  if [ "$D" = "RESULT: PASSED" ] && [ "$K" -ge 1 ] && [ "$V" -ge 1 ]; then echo "C2 PASS draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; else echo "C2 FAIL draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; fi
   561	
   562	  # C3: the frozen phase 3 census, rerun on the final tree.
   563	  ORCH="$S/runtime/dist/lib/validation/orchestrator.js"
   564	  for i in 0 1 2; do env -u SPECKIT_RULES node "$P3/scratch/tools/strict-census.mjs" "$ORCH" "$P3/scratch/census/shard-$i.txt" > "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.tsv" 2> "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.err" & done
   565	  wait
   566	  cat "$SC/closeout/b-final-"[012].tsv | LC_ALL=C sort > "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv"
   567	  cut -f1,2,5 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut"
   568	  cut -f1,2,5 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut"
   569	  DIFFROWS=$(diff "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut" "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut" | grep -c '^[<>]')
   570	  BCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
   571	  FCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
   572	  if [ "$DIFFROWS" = "0" ]; then echo "C3 PASS CENSUS_SAME gate B before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; else echo "C3 FAIL diff_rows=$DIFFROWS before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; fi
   573	
   574	  # C4: the two guards.
   575	  AOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-example-drift.sh 2>&1); AR=$?
   576	  A=$(printf '%s\n' "$AOUT" | tail -1)
   577	  BOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-card-sync.sh 2>&1); BR=$?
   578	  B=$(printf '%s\n' "$BOUT" | tail -1)
   579	  if [ "$A" = "EXAMPLE_DRIFT_OK" ] && [ "$B" = "CARD SYNC OK" ] && [ "$AR" = "0" ] && [ "$BR" = "0" ]; then echo "C4 PASS $A $B" >> "$C"; else echo "C4 FAIL $A rc=$AR $B rc=$BR" >> "$C"; fi
   580	
   581	  # C5: the report covers five metrics on two sides of three runs, and no final run regressed against the worst baseline run.
   582	  N=$(jq '.metrics | length' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
   583	  BRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$BL/baseline.json")
   584	  FRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json")
   585	  LOW=$(jq -r '([.metrics[] | select(.metric=="leftover_hints")][0]) | "\(.baseline.max) -> \(.final.max)"' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
   586	  OK=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "yes" else "no" end' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
   587	  if [ "$N" = "5" ] && [ "$BRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$FRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$OK" = "yes" ]; then echo "C5 PASS metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; else echo "C5 FAIL metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; fi
   588	
   589	  # C6: every playbook scenario this packet added has a PASS or an escalated FAIL.
   590	  REPORTS="$S/benchmark/reports"
   591	  REPORT=$(for d in "$REPORTS"/*--manual-testing-playbook--*/; do test -s "$d/source.md" && rg -q '054-weak-model-spec-authoring' "$d/source.md" && echo "$d"; done | sort | tail -1)
   592	  ADDED=$({ git diff --name-only "$START" -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; } | sort -u | rg '\.md$' | rg -v 'manual-testing-playbook\.md$')
   593	  MISS=0; LINES=""
   594	  if [ -z "$REPORT" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL MISSING-008-REPORT" >> "$C"; elif [ -z "$ADDED" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL NO_SCENARIO_FILES" >> "$C"; else
   595	    for f in $ADDED; do
   596	      SLUG=$(basename "$(dirname "$f")")-$(basename "$f" .md)
   597	      NUM=$(rg -m1 -o '^title: "([0-9]+)' -r '$1' "$f")
   598	      V=$(awk -F, -v id="$SLUG" -v num="$NUM" 'NR==1{for(i=1;i<=NF;i++){if($i=="scenario_id")s=i; if($i=="verdict")v=i}; next} ($s==id || $s==num){print $v}' "$REPORT/results.csv" | head -1)
   599	      if [ "$V" != "PASS" ]; then
   600	        if rg -q 'FAIL' "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" && { rg -q --fixed-strings "$SLUG" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" || rg -q --fixed-strings "$NUM" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md"; }; then V="FAIL-LOGGED"; else MISS=$((MISS+1)); fi
   601	      fi
   602	      LINES="$LINES $SLUG=$V"
   603	    done
   604	    if [ "$MISS" = "0" ]; then echo "C6 PASS$LINES" >> "$C"; else echo "C6 FAIL missing=$MISS$LINES" >> "$C"; fi
   605	  fi
   606	
   607	  # C7: swarm commits stay local and nothing is pushed.
   608	  HEAD=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
   609	  COUNT=$(git log --oneline "$START..HEAD" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   610	  REMOTE=$(git branch -r --contains HEAD | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   611	  if [ "$COUNT" = "0" ] || [ "$REMOTE" = "0" ]; then echo "C7 PASS head=$HEAD commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; else echo "C7 FAIL head=$HEAD start=$START commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; fi
   612	
   613	  cat "$C"
   614	  PASSES=$(grep -c '^C[1-7] PASS ' "$C")
   615	  N="$PASSES" perl -0pi -e 's{^(\| BASE_REF \|.*\n)}{$1 . "| Parent criteria | Done | $ENV{N} PASS lines in scratch/closeout/criteria.txt |\n"}me unless /\| Parent criteria \|/' "$PKT/goal.md"
   616	  echo "CRITERIA_LOGGED passes=$PASSES"
   617	  ```

   683	  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PHASE/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[x\]'
   684	  ```

    69	
    70	### Status values
    71	
    72	| Value | Meaning |
    73	|-------|---------|
    74	| `Met` | Verified. The Verification cell names evidence that was actually observed. |
    75	| `Unmet` | Not yet satisfied. Blocks closure. |
    76	| `Waived` | Deliberately not pursued. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |
    77	| `Superseded` | Replaced by a different criterion or decision. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |
    78	
    79	### Waiver cell
    80	
    81	Write `-` when the row is `Met` or `Unmet`. Write `ADR-NNN` when the row is
    82	`Waived` or `Superseded`, naming a decision record that exists in
    83	`decision-record.md`. A waiver naming an ADR that is not there fails validation:
    84	the point of a waiver is that someone recorded the reasoning, so an unbacked
    85	waiver is treated as an unmet criterion rather than as a pass.

115-| Run protocol on 2026-10-25 | The runs mirror the script that produced the SWE 2 Max baseline. Each dispatch uses the baseline brief-1.txt copied byte for byte (cmp), runs under a 2700 second watchdog on cli-devin swe-2-max with --permission-mode dangerous, and records its exit status. M5 is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, as in the baseline, not the measurement time. M2 is the bare hint count (hints-l3.txt) plus the value token count. M4 is the status diff, and a change in the hash of git diff -- .skilled before and after a run also counts as an out-of-scope write. Each run's packet is copied to the scratchpad outside the worktree before it is removed. The session audit selects Devin sessions in the worktree between the start second and the end second plus 60, requires exactly one, and searches both tool_call_json and tool_call_update_json. A rate-limit or billing reply is not a run and is redone for the same run number. Extra per-run fields (exit status, bare and token split, audit) are in scratch/baseline/runs-meta.jsonl so baseline.json keeps the six keys per run. |

115-| Run protocol on 2026-10-25 | The runs mirror the script that produced the SWE 2 Max baseline. Each dispatch uses the baseline brief-1.txt copied byte for byte (cmp), runs under a 2700 second watchdog on cli-devin swe-2-max with --permission-mode dangerous, and records its exit status. M5 is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, as in the baseline, not the measurement time. M2 is the bare hint count (hints-l3.txt) plus the value token count. M4 is the status diff, and a change in the hash of git diff -- .skilled before and after a run also counts as an out-of-scope write. Each run's packet is copied to the scratchpad outside the worktree before it is removed. The session audit selects Devin sessions in the worktree between the start second and the end second plus 60, requires exactly one, and searches both tool_call_json and tool_call_update_json. A rate-limit or billing reply is not a run and is redone for the same run number. Extra per-run fields (exit status, bare and token split, audit) are in scratch/baseline/runs-meta.jsonl so baseline.json keeps the six keys per run. |

/bin/zsh -lc 'cat specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/amend3/review-input/justified-anchors.sh' in /Users/michelkerkmeester/MEGA/Development/Code_Environment/Public/.worktrees/217-weak-model-spec-authoring
 succeeded in 0ms:
#!/bin/bash
# usage: justified-anchors.sh <packet-dir> [worktree-root]
# Scores one authored packet under the anchor rule of parent criterion 5.
# A frozen anchor missing from a packet document counts, unless it is an optional
# Level 3 tasks.md anchor in the frozen contract and its fill-note condition holds.
#   arch-verify       excused when spec.md, plan.md and decision-record.md hold no ADR-<n>
#   perf-verify       excused when spec.md holds no NFR-P<n>
#   deploy-ready, compliance-verify, sign-off   excused by task definition
#   docs-verify       never excused
# Any missing anchor in spec.md, plan.md or acceptance-criteria.md counts, and so does a required tasks.md anchor.
# Output: counted=<n> excused=<list> counted_list=<list>
D=${1:?packet dir}
ROOT=${2:-$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../../../../../.." && pwd)}
P1=$ROOT/specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/001-template-drift-census-and-baseline
P9=$ROOT/specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout
FROZEN=$P1/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/anchors-l3.txt
CONTRACT=$P9/scratch/closeout/contract-3-tasks.json
GREP=/usr/bin/grep
OPT=$(jq -r ".optionalAnchors[]" "$CONTRACT" | tr "\n" " ")
REQ=$(jq -r ".requiredAnchors[]" "$CONTRACT" | tr "\n" " ")
has_adr=no; cat "$D/spec.md" "$D/plan.md" "$D/decision-record.md" 2>/dev/null | $GREP -qE 'ADR-[0-9]+' && has_adr=yes
has_nfr=no; $GREP -qE 'NFR-P[0-9]+' "$D/spec.md" 2>/dev/null && has_nfr=yes
counted=0; excused=""; clist=""
while read -r doc want; do
  [ "$doc" = tasks ] && continue
  have=$($GREP -c '<!-- ANCHOR:' "$D/$doc.md" 2>/dev/null); have=${have:-0}
  if [ "$have" -lt "$want" ]; then s=$((want - have)); counted=$((counted + s)); clist="$clist $doc(-$s)"; fi
done < "$FROZEN"
for a in $REQ $OPT; do
  $GREP -q "<!-- ANCHOR:$a -->" "$D/tasks.md" 2>/dev/null && continue
  ok=no
  case $a in
    arch-verify) [ "$has_adr" = no ] && ok=yes;;
    perf-verify) [ "$has_nfr" = no ] && ok=yes;;
    deploy-ready|compliance-verify|sign-off) ok=yes;;
  esac
  case " $OPT " in *" $a "*) ;; *) ok=no;; esac
  if [ $ok = yes ]; then excused="$excused $a"; else counted=$((counted + 1)); clist="$clist $a"; fi
done
echo "counted=$counted excused=[${excused# }] counted_list=[${clist# }]"

The rule, fixed before any fresh run: a frozen anchor missing from a final packet counts unless it is optional at Level 3 in `template-structure.js contract 3 tasks.md` and its fill-note condition holds for that packet. arch-verify holds when no `ADR-<n>` appears in the packet's spec.md, plan.md or decision-record.md. perf-verify holds when spec.md has no `NFR-P<n>`. deploy-ready, compliance-verify and sign-off hold by task definition, because the frozen brief adds a flag to one shell script, ships no service, changes no license, dependency or data handling and names no approver. docs-verify never holds, because every Level 3 packet pairs spec, plan and tasks. Three fresh final runs decide the box, run in a disposable sk-git worktree whose working tree holds none of this packet's files. Both earlier final sets stay on record and are scored under both rules: the swe-2-max set scores 3 under the new rule (run 3 deleted arch-verify, perf-verify and docs-verify while recording ADR-001 and NFR-P01), and the second DeepSeek set scores 0 but cannot decide the box after its result was seen |
